Result: 使用内在指标（语义多样性、摘要质量、偏见减少）评估各组件，显示出优于强基线的改进，同时保持内容忠实度；提供公开可访问的实时演示以促进可复现性和进一步研究。

Conclusion: AutoJourn为社会负责的自动化新闻提供了一个综合系统，展示了LLMs在多视角新闻生成和偏见缓解方面的潜力，并通过公开演示推动了该领域的可复现研究。

Abstract: We present AutoJourn, a demonstration system for multi-perspective news generation and bias-aware evaluation using large language models (LLMs). The system tackles three core challenges in responsible automated journalism: extracting diverse perspectives from unstructured social media discussions, generating summaries that preserve viewpoint diversity, and detecting or mitigating bias in AI-generated news. The pipeline integrates advanced prompt engineering with optional retrieval augmentation to produce semantically diverse perspective sets, a multi-perspective summarisation module that merges conflicting viewpoints into balanced summaries, and a bias analysis suite supporting sentence-level bias detection and type classification in the generated news article, and automatic neutralisation. Users can inspect perspective clusters, compare stance-specific summaries, generate news articles, and apply bias-aware rewrites directly in the interface. We evaluate each component with intrinsic metrics -- semantic diversity, summary quality, and bias reduction and show improvements over strong baselines while maintaining content fidelity. A live, publicly accessible demo accompanies the paper to facilitate reproducibility and further research on socially responsible automated journalism.

</details>


### [26] [Disentangling Curriculum Learning in NLP: Towards a Unifying Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18984)
*Vanessa Toborek,Florian Seiffarth,Sebastian Müller,Tamás Horváth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度的课程学习分类法，将难度评估与训练调度分离，系统分析了NLP领域的CL策略，揭示了现有工作中难度概念与调度策略的混淆问题，并为CL策略的设计、分析和比较提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管课程学习在NLP领域研究已超过十年，但缺乏关于为何选择特定难度函数或调度器的原则性解释，且现有工作难以系统比较和积累连贯的证据基础。

Method: 提出细粒度分类法：将难度评估拆解为归因来源和任务依赖性，将调度形式化为期望训练贡献，引入保留机制和单调性属性，并以此分析NLP领域的CL文献。

Result: 分类法揭示了系统性的不可比性问题：先前工作混淆了不同难度概念与调度策略，常在同一CL标签下追求不同目标，阻碍了比较和证据积累。

Conclusion: 该分类法不仅诊断了现有问题，还支持CL策略的设计、分析和比较，并激励能够分离改进来源的评估实践。

Abstract: Despite more than a decade of curriculum learning (CL) research in NLP, the field lacks a principled account of which difficulty function or scheduler to use for a given problem. To understand what has hindered progress towards this account, we propose a fine-grained taxonomy separating difficulty evaluation from training scheduling to enable systematic analysis of CL strategies. For difficulty evaluation, we distinguish attribution source and task dependence, revealing difficulty as a perspectival concept encoding different assumptions about what makes an instance hard to learn. For scheduling, we provide the first formalisation of CL schedulers in terms of expected training contribution, enabling comparison across implementations by introducing retention regimes and monotonicity properties. Applied in a dedicated analysis of CL works in NLP, our taxonomy reveals a systematic incomparability problem: prior works conflate distinct notions of difficulty and scheduling, often pursuing different objectives under the same CL label -- hindering comparison and the accumulation of a coherent evidence base. Beyond diagnosis, the taxonomy supports the design, analysis, and comparison of CL strategies, and motivates evaluation practices that disentangle the sources of observed improvement.

</details>


### [27] [MedDDC-Eval: Diagnosis-Decoupled Evaluation of Multi-Turn Medical Consultation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18999)
*Guofeng Zhang,Yizeng Quan,Huaiyi Fang,Jianwei Lv,Jinyao Liu,Xunxu Duan,Lening An,Yu Ouyang,Junfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedDDC-Eval，一种解耦诊断生成的多轮医疗咨询评测基准，通过冻结共享的历史到诊断映射（reader）来单独评估策略采集病史的质量，并结合 GRPO 进行基于诊断结果和轨迹反馈的策略后训练，显著提升了证据采集效率与诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的耦合评测将策略采集的病史质量与特定策略的终端诊断生成混淆：强生成能力可掩盖稀疏病史，弱生成能力可能掩盖丰富病史，导致无法准确归因策略本身的证据采集能力。

Method: 1. 构建 MedDDC-Eval：将采集的病史作为比较对象，使用共享冻结的 reader 固定历史到诊断的映射，消除生成能力的干扰。2. 引入 grounded 接口和可审计的诊断-轨迹-效率（D/T/E）评测 harness，测量诊断有用性、信息获取量与效率。3. 采用方向性语义覆盖加确定性一对一分配，为开放式项目生成连贯的精确率-召回率计数。4. 应用标准 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 在交互式多轮 rollout 上对 Qwen3-32B 进行后训练，利用诊断结果与轨迹反馈作为奖励信号。

Result: 固定病史仅更换 reader 即导致诊断 F1 波动 2.2-19.0 点，并在 Record 与 Dialogue 分裂上分别反转 18% 和 36% 的策略成对排序。GRPO 后训练使策略在 100 例 Record 与 70 例 Dialogue 上的总分分别提升 9.7 与 4.6 点；移除诊断结果或轨迹任一主要信号均降低举出联合性能。

Conclusion: MedDDC-Eval 实现了受控归因、可解释的病史采集度量，并支持基于评测引导的证据采集策略开发，为多轮医疗咨询代理的研发提供了可靠的评测与优化框架。

Abstract: Multi-turn medical consultation agents must decide what to ask, adapt to patient responses, and determine when the collected evidence is sufficient. However, coupled evaluation conflates the quality of the policy-elicited history with policy-specific terminal diagnosis generation: strong generation can compensate for a thin history, while weaker generation can obscure a rich one. We introduce MedDDC-Eval, a diagnosis-decoupled testbed that treats elicited history as the comparison object and holds the history-to-diagnosis mapping constant through a shared frozen reader. Across two held-out sources, a grounded interface and an auditable diagnosis-trajectory-efficiency (D/T/E) harness measure diagnostic usefulness, information acquisition, and efficiency. Directional semantic coverage followed by deterministic one-to-one assignment yields coherent precision-recall counts for open-ended items, with at most one credited match per prediction or reference. Holding histories fixed, changing only the diagnostic reader shifts diagnosis F1 by 2.2-19.0 points and reverses 18% and 36% of pairwise policy orderings on the Record and Dialogue splits. We further apply standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) over interactive multi-turn rollouts to post-train Qwen3-32B using diagnosis-result and trajectory feedback. On the 100-case Record and 70-case Dialogue splits, the trained policy improves over its initialization by 9.7 and 4.6 total-score points; removing either primary signal lowers held-out joint performance. These results show that MedDDC-Eval supports controlled attribution, interpretable elicited-history measurement, and evaluation-guided evidence-acquisition policy development.

</details>


### [28] [Computational Humor with Multimodal LLMs: Methods, Datasets, Evaluation, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19011)
*Tuo Liang,Zhe Hu,Disheng Liu,Jing Li,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了多模态视觉幽默理解领域，聚焦单图与多格漫画中的幽默识别、推理与生成，梳理了从任务专用融合模型到大模型对齐、证据推理与可控生成的演进，指出评测捷径、文化覆盖不足、证据锚定薄弱及安全版权问题为主要阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统难以处理梗图、漫画等多模态幽默，因其依赖非字面机制、共享文化知识与交际意图，而非字面场景描述；先前综述未系统覆盖视觉幽默的识别、推理与生成全链路，亟需能力导向的层级化梳理。

Method: 采用能力中心层级框架（识别、解释推理、生成）组织文献，对比既有幽默、讽刺及通用MLLM综述，综合基准设计、评测协议与建模范式，追踪从任务专用融合模型向多模态对齐、证据锚定推理与可控生成大模型的范式转移。

Result: 梳理了视觉幽默理解的三层能力体系与主流基准/模型，揭示领域已从小模型融合转向大模型对齐与推理，但仍面临评测易被捷径利用、文化与叙事覆盖有限、证据锚定薄弱、安全与版权未决四大核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 多模态视觉幽默理解虽随大模型推进显著，但需解决评测鲁棒性、文化叙事广度、证据级推理深度及安全版权合规，未来应推动更具挑战性的基准构建、跨文化知识注入、可解释推理机制与负责任生成框架的协同发展。

Abstract: Multimodal humor in memes, cartoons, and comics remains difficult for AI systems because intended meaning depends on non-literal mechanisms, shared cultural knowledge, and communicative intent rather than literal scene description. This survey focuses on visual humor understanding in single-image and multi-panel artifacts, while treating humor generation as an emerging downstream frontier. We position the literature against prior humor, sarcasm, and general MLLM surveys and organize it using a capability-centric hierarchy spanning recognition, interpretation and reasoning, and generation. Under this lens, we synthesize benchmark design, evaluation protocols, and modeling paradigms, tracing the field's shift from task-specific fusion models to large-model approaches based on multimodal alignment, evidence-grounded reasoning, and controlled generation. We conclude by highlighting the main barriers to progress: shortcut-prone evaluation, limited cultural and narrative coverage, weak evidence grounding, and unresolved safety and ownership concerns.

</details>


### [29] [Content is What Remains: Invariant Speech Tokenization from Parallel Utterances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19033)
*Laurin Wagner,Bernhard Thallinger,Miroslav Stankovic,Mario Zusag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PINT 通过在并行语音对上对齐 SSL 编码器，将非语言因素（说话人、韵律、信道）从离散语音 token 中剥离，实现了 98.7% 的说话人探测准确率下降、42% 的 ABX 错误率降低以及 27-30% 的语言模型困惑度减低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习（SSL）语音 token（如 HuBERT）保留了说话人身份、韵律和信道条件等非语言变异，导致 token 熵值虚高，难以作为高效的语义表示。

Method: 提出 PINT（Parallel INvariant Tokenization），利用多说话人朗读相同文本的并行语料，配合数据增强，通过对齐损失微调 SSL 编码器，提取共享的语言成分，使相同词汇映射为一致的 token 序列，同时保留帧级时序对齐。

Result: 说话人探测准确率从 93.1% 降至 1.2%（相对下降 98.7%），ABX 错误率降低 42%，语言模型困惑度降低 27-30%，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 在并行语音上施加恰当的不变性约束是学习高效、低熵语义语音 token 的关键。

Abstract: Discrete speech tokenizers aim to disentangle semantic from acoustic information, yet targets from self-supervised learning (SSL) models like HuBERT retain non-linguistic variation: speaker identity, prosody, and channel conditions leak into the tokens, inflating entropy. Our key insight is that when enough speakers utter the same words under varying conditions, linguistic content is the only shared factor. We propose PINT (Parallel INvariant Tokenization), which fine-tunes an SSL encoder with alignment losses across parallel utterances and augmentations to distill this shared residual. PINT collapses identical words onto consistent token sequences, drastically reducing conditional entropy. Unlike ASR text, PINT tokens preserve frame-level temporal grounding and serve as drop-in semantic targets for audio codecs. Experiments show a 98.7% relative reduction in speaker probe accuracy (93.1% to 1.2%), a 42% lower ABX error rate, and 27-30% lower LM perplexity versus baselines, confirming that the right invariance is key to efficient learning.

</details>


### [30] [Benchmarking Human and Automatic Speech Recognition of Diverse Speech: Initial Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19049)
*Ilse Huisman,Rares Popa,Yuanyuan Zhang,Odette Scharenborg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了最先进的ASR系统与荷兰母语听众在识别多样化语音（儿童、老年人及佛兰德语）上的表现。Google Telephony表现最佳，ASR整体性能与人类相当甚至超越，但存在与年龄、口音、语句长度相关的细微差异。研究揭示测试集选择对人机基准测试结论的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前最先进的自动语音识别（ASR）系统是否已达到或超越人类听众在识别多样化语音（包括儿童语音、老年语音和区域口音语音）上的表现，并分析影响性能差异的因素。

Method: 选取最先进的ASR系统（包括Google Telephony）与荷兰母语听众进行对比实验，使用Jasmin-CGN语料库中的测试刺激集及完整测试集，评估对荷兰儿童语音、老年语音及佛兰德语的识别性能，分析不同年龄、口音、语句长度条件下的表现差异。

Result: Google Telephony在ASR系统中表现最佳；ASR系统整体性能与人类听众相近，在特定情况下甚至优于人类；性能差异与说话人年龄、区域口音及语句长度相关；测试集的选择（特定测试刺激集 vs 完整Jasmin-CGN测试集）会显著影响人机基准测试的结论。

Conclusion: 当前ASR系统在多样化语音识别上已接近或超越人类水平，但针对衰老相关的声学变异性和区域口音的鲁棒性仍需提升；未来研究应关注增强ASR对这些变异的适应能力，且基准测试需考虑测试集选择对结论的影响。

Abstract: Humans are often considered to be the best listeners and seen as the upper-bound performance of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. We present a preliminary comparison of the performances of state-of-the-art ASR systems and Dutch native listeners on the recognition of "diverse" speech, specifically Dutch child and older adults' speech and Flemish. Google Telephony outperformed the other ASR systems. Importantly, the ASR systems showed similar performance to the listeners, and in specific cases even outperformed them. Slight performance differences between the listeners and ASR systems were found related to speaker's age and regional accents and utterance length. Future research should focus on making ASR systems more robust to acoustic variability related to aging and regional accents. A comparison of ASR recognition performances on the test stimuli and the full Jasmin-CGN test sets showed the influence of the specific test sets on the conclusions regarding benchmarking human and ASR performance.

</details>


### [31] [DAIS: Dependency-Aware Intermediate QA Supervision for Complex Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19088)
*Yu Wang,Ming Fan,Xicheng Zhang,Zhiyong Li,Zhihu Wang,Caiyue Xu,Dahai Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DAIS框架将教师推理转化为分阶段QA记录，通过依赖感知的中间监督提升最终答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT监督仅优化单一推理序列，缺乏对局部结论如何支撑后续决策的监督。

Method: 提出DAIS训练时框架，将过滤后的教师推理转换为阶段级QA记录，每个中间记录基于先前状态预测局部答案，最终答案保持原任务格式。

Result: 在GDPR、AIACT、MedQA、FOLIO等基准上，DAIS平均提升最终答案准确率；政策合规基准上最大增益5.6%，平均增益4.2%；消融实验验证前置状态条件化的有效性。

Conclusion: 依赖条件化的中间QA作为轻量级辅助监督信号，能有效提升标准最终答案推理性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision exposes intermediate rationales, but flat rationale targets usually optimize a single reasoning sequence and provide limited supervision on how local conclusions should support later decisions. We introduce Dependency-Aware Intermediate QA Supervision (DAIS), a training-time framework that converts filtered teacher rationales into stage-level QA records. Each intermediate record predicts a local answer conditioned on the previous states needed for that decision, while the final-answer record keeps the original task format; evaluation therefore uses only the original input and optional context. Across GDPR, AIACT, MedQA, and FOLIO with multiple Qwen backbones, DAIS improves average final-answer accuracy over answer-only, flat chain-of-thought, and independent-QA baselines. On policy-compliance benchmarks, it achieves a largest gain of 5.6% and an average gain of 4.2% over the strongest non-DAIS baseline. Controlled ablations show that valid previous-state conditioning contributes beyond longer targets or additional intermediate text, supporting dependency-conditioned intermediate QA as a lightweight auxiliary supervision signal for standard final-answer inference.

</details>


### [32] [Translation as Augmentation: Effect of Translated Data on Assessment of Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19101)
*Yiheng Wu,Jue Hou,Roman Yangarber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言文本难度评估中专家标注数据稀缺的问题，提出跨语言数据增强策略，利用机器翻译将高资源语言的标注数据迁移至目标低资源语言，训练BERT回归模型预测难度分数，实验证明合成翻译数据能显著提升难度估计准确性，为缺乏专家标注的语言提供可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的文本难度评估是文本简化工作流和个性化学习应用的前提，但细粒度难度等级（如CEFR）的专家标注语料极度稀缺，尤其是低资源语言，严重阻碍了鲁棒评估模型的开发。

Method: 提出跨语言数据增强策略，利用机器翻译将高资源语言的标注资源迁移到目标低资源语言；训练基于BERT的回归模型预测难度分数，探究合成翻译数据能否有效补充原生训练集。

Result: 实验表明，用机器翻译语料增稀疏原生数据能显著提高难度估计的准确性。

Conclusion: 跨语言数据增强为缺乏大量专家标注的低资源语言提供了可行的文本难度评估解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable Text Difficulty Assessment is a prerequisite for valid text simplification workflows and personalized learning applications. However, the development of robust assessment models is severely hindered by a critical bottleneck: the scarcity of expert-annotated corpora containing fine-grained difficulty levels (e.g., CEFR), particularly for lower-resource languages. This paper addresses this data scarcity problem in the context of a low-resource European language. We propose a cross-lingual data augmentation strategy that leverages machine translation to transfer labeled resources from high-resource languages to the target low-resource language. We train BERT-based regression models to predict difficulty scores and investigate whether synthetic, translated data can effectively supplement native training sets. Our experiments demonstrate that augmenting scarce native data with machine-translated corpora significantly improves the accuracy of difficulty estimation, offering a viable solution for languages lacking extensive expert annotations.

</details>


### [33] [Automated Extraction of Techno-Economic Data from 76,000 Energy System Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19178)
*Maxime Gorres,Jan Göpfert,Patrick Kuckertz,Noor Titan Putri Hartono,Heidi Heinrichs,Jochen Linßen,Iain Staffel,Jann Michael Weinand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于76,000篇能源系统文献的自动化提取数据库，包含320万结构化定量数据点和2000万元数据条目，实现了FAIR原则，并通过交互式仪表板支持大规模元分析，揭示了学术假设与实证数据的偏差及研究优先级的时空演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统模型依赖难以溯源和审计的定量假设，手工元分析因文献爆炸性增长而变得不切实际，现有数据库更新滞后且存在重复劳动，亟需自动化、可复用的大规模文献信息提取方案。

Method: 采用高精度自动化信息提取技术，处理2010年以来发表的76,000篇能源系统研究论文，抽取320万结构化定量数据点及2000万相关元数据条目，涵盖技术、方法、系统特征等多维度，构建符合FAIR原则的数据库并开发交互式仪表板。

Result: 生成的数据库使能源系统文献本身可被大规模分析：揭示学术假设与实证观测数据的系统性偏离，量化不同技术、地区、时间维度的研究优先级演变，并提供可筛选、可下载的交互式分析工具供社区使用。

Conclusion: 自动化文献挖掘能显著提升能源建模的透明度与可复现性，减少重复劳动，支持跨尺度的证据综合，为政策相关的能源决策提供更可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: Energy system models guide societally important decisions, but their credibility rests on quantitative assumptions that are difficult to source and audit. Meta-analyses can improve transparency and modeling practices, but the rapid growth of publications makes manual information extraction increasingly impractical. Consequently, databases are updated infrequently and efforts are often duplicated across research groups. Here, we demonstrate the highly accurate automated extraction of quantitative information from 76,000 energy system studies published since 2010. We compile 3.2 million structured quantitative data points together with 20 million associated metadata entries, spanning a broad spectrum of technologies, methodological approaches and system characteristics. Beyond providing input data for models, the resulting FAIR database make the energy systems literature itself analysable. We show where academic assumptions diverge from empirical observed data, and how research priorities vary at scale across technologies, regions and time. To facilitate broad use within the community, the database is provided through an interactive dashboard, enabling users to filter, analyse and download data according to their specific research needs.

</details>


### [34] [Reasoning Before Translation: Enhancing Legal Machine Translation with Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19181)
*Aixiu An,Michael Jungo,Eloi Eynard,Mark Drenhaus,Andreas Fischer,Jean Hennebert,Sébastien Rumley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了法律领域神经机器翻译（NMT）中小模型（Qwen3.5 4B/9B, Gemma 3 12B）经监督微调（SFT）与可验证奖励强化学习（RL）增强后的表现，并与前沿推理模型对比。实验基于瑞士多语言法规体系。结果显示：小模型经增强质量大幅提升；RL 优于 SFT；增强后小模型接近但仍逊于最先进推理模型；再训练收益随模型规模增大而递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律翻译因语言复杂性和高精度要求极具挑战。近期推理能力强的语言模型为解决此类问题提供新思路，此前已有监督微调、强化学习等技术提升翻译质量。本文旨在系统对比这些方法在法律 NMT 中的效果，并以瑞士多语言法规为高难度测试床。

Method: 选取 Qwen3.5 4B、Qwen3.5 9B、Gemma 3 12B 等小模型，分别应用监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）等再训练范式进行增强。将增强后的小模型与前沿推理模型在瑞士法律文本翻译任务上进行对比评估。

Result: 1) 小基座模型经再训练质量显著提升。2) 可验证奖励 RL 可应用于法律 NMT 且超越 SFT。3) 增强后小模型性能接近最先进推理模型，但仍略逊一筹。4) 再训练范式的收益随模型规模增大呈边际递减趋势。

Conclusion: 可验证奖励强化学习是提升法律领域 NMT 质量的有效手段，优于监督微调。小模型经增强可大幅缩小与前沿推理模型的差距，但仍无法完全匹敌。随着模型规模增长，再训练带来的边际收益递减，提示未来研究需探索更高效的大模型适配策略。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) in the legal domain is a linguistically and conceptually demanding task, primarily due to the complexity of legal language and the high level of precision it requires. The recent emergence of reasoning-capable language models opens new possibilities for tackling such challenges. They add to a set of other previously proposed techniques to enhance the translation quality, which includes supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
  In this work, we perform a comparison between these various approaches. More particularly, we evaluate small language models such as Qwen3.5 4B, Qwen3.5 9B, and Gemma 3 12B enhanced with various re-training paradigms and compare their performances against frontier reasoning models. We focus on the Swiss legal system, which -- with its unique multilingual statutes -- offers a particularly challenging testbed for reasoning-augmented models. Our results show that the quality of small ``base'' models can be greatly enhanced, and that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can be applied to NMT in the legal domain and surpasses the translation quality of supervised fine-tuning. The performance of enhanced small models is close to the one of state-of-the-art reasoning models yet remains inferior. We also note that re-training paradigms yield diminishing returns as model size increase. The code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/aixiuxiuxiu/Legal-MT-SFT-RL.

</details>


### [35] [MIRA-Ev:A Benchmark for Granular Evidence Detection and Relational Reasoning in Clinical Exams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19201)
*Iker De la Iglesia,Johanna Ramirez-Romero,Jose Maria Villa-Gonzalez,Irune Urroz García,Ander Barrena,Aitziber Atutxa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIRA-Ev，一个基于西班牙 MIR 执照考试病例的临床论证挖掘基准，包含专家标注的跨度级前提、主张及支持/攻击关系，提供西班牙语、英语和巴斯克语三语版本，首创巴斯克语临床论证资源，并将评估组织为三层任务：证据句检索、论证成分抽取、关系分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床 NLP 评估主要依赖多项选择题答题（MCQA），仅评估最终答案准确性，无法检测模型是否基于无关、缺失或矛盾证据得出正确诊断。

Method: 利用西班牙 MIR 执照考试病例，由专家临床医生重新标注跨度级前提、主张及有向支持/攻击关系，构建多语言（西班牙语、英语、巴斯克语）并行语料，设计三层评估任务体系。

Result: 发布了 MIRA-Ev 基准，包含三语版本，首创巴斯克语临床论证资源，提供证据句检索、论证成分抽取、关系分类三层评估任务。

Conclusion: MIRA-Ev 解决了 MCQA 评估的局限，为临床 NLP 模型的证据推理能力提供了细粒度、多语言的评估基准。

Abstract: Clinical NLP evaluation remains dominated by multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), which scores only final-answer accuracy and cannot detect when a model reaches the correct diagnosis while grounding it in irrelevant, absent, or contradictory evidence. We introduce MIRA-Ev, a clinical argument mining benchmark built on Spanish Médico Interno Residente (MIR) licensing-exam cases, re-annotated by expert clinicians with span-level premises, claims, and directed support/attack relations, and released in parallel Spanish (native), English, and Basque versions, the first clinical argumentation resource in Basque. MIRA-Ev organizes evaluation into a three-tier task hierarchy: evidence sentence retrieval, argumentative component extraction, and relation classification.

</details>


### [36] [Beyond Score Prediction: LLM-Based Essay Scoring and Feedback Generation via Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19219)
*Xuefeng Jin,Jiashuo Zhang,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RLAES框架，利用强化学习联合优化自动作文评分（AES）与自动反馈生成（AFG），引入基于评分细则的反馈评估（RFE）包含166个细粒度二元评分项和LLM-as-judge，提出自适应门控反馈优化（AGFO）按需激活反馈奖励降低开销，以及相邻对比推理（ACR）改善序数分数校准。在ASAP基准上，RLAES-AGFO在LLM方法中得分最佳（QWK=0.803），反馈质量媲美GPT-5.5且避免了仅分数RL导致的反馈退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES和AFG研究主要依赖提示工程或监督微调，缺乏系统性的强化学习后训练研究和反馈质量自动评估方法，导致难以联合优化评分与反馈生成，且仅优化分数会导致反馈质量下降。

Method: 1. 提出RLAES统一LLM框架，通过RL联合优化评分与反馈生成。2. 引入RFE评估框架：166个细粒度二元评分项+LLM-as-judge，使反馈质量可测量、可解释、可用于训练。3. 提出AGFO：在RL过程中按需激活基于评分细则的反馈奖励，降低评估开销并提升反馈质量。4. 提出ACR：通过显式对比相邻分数等级改善序数分数校准。

Result: RFE框架能捕捉作文-反馈一致性，具有强判别力且与专家偏好高度对齐。在ASAP基准上，RLAES-AGFO在基于LLM的方法中取得最佳评分性能（QWK=0.803），反馈质量媲美GPT-5.5，且避免了仅优化分数的RL导致的反馈退化问题。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: RLAES通过RL后训练实现了AES与AFG的联合优化，RFE提供了可靠的反馈质量评估，AGFO在降低开销的同时提升反馈质量，ACR改善了分数校准。该方法在评分性能上超越现有LLM基线，且保持了高质量反馈生成，为自动作文评分与反馈生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely applied to automated essay scoring (AES) and automated feedback generation (AFG). However, existing studies rely primarily on prompt engineering or supervised fine-tuning, while systematic research on reinforcement learning (RL) post-training and automated evaluation of feedback quality remains limited. We propose RLAES, a unified LLM framework that jointly optimizes essay scoring and feedback generation through RL. To make feedback quality measurable, interpretable, and usable for training, we introduce Rubric-based Feedback Evaluation (RFE), an essay-grounded feedback evaluation framework comprising 166 fine-grained binary rubric items and an LLM-as-judge. Building on RFE, we propose Adaptive Gated Feedback Optimization (AGFO), which activates rubric-based feedback rewards on demand during RL, reducing evaluation overhead while improving feedback quality. We also propose Adjacent Contrastive Reasoning (ACR) to improve ordinal score calibration by explicitly contrasting adjacent score levels. Experimental results show that the RFE framework captures essay-feedback consistency, exhibits strong pairwise discriminative power, and closely aligns with expert preferences. On the ASAP benchmark, RLAES-AGFO achieves the best scoring performance among LLM-based methods (QWK = 0.803), while maintaining feedback quality comparable to GPT-5.5 and avoiding the feedback degradation observed under score-only RL. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/hellomuyi/RLAES.

</details>


### [37] [The Price of Reasoning: Cost-Quality Tradeoffs in Reinforcement Learning for Neural Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19226)
*Michael Jungo,Aixiu An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）用于法律文档神经机器翻译时，推理轨迹在训练和推理阶段的作用。实验表明，在推理阶段包含推理轨迹能提升翻译质量，但会增加输出token数量，需权衡计算成本与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RLVR在法律文档翻译中的有效性是归因于推理能力还是训练范式本身，以及推理轨迹在训练和推理阶段的具体贡献。

Method: 系统地在训练或推理阶段省略模型的推理轨迹，对比包含推理轨迹与不包含时的翻译质量，并分析输出token增加带来的计算成本与质量提升的权衡。

Result: 在推理阶段包含推理轨迹对整体翻译质量有正面影响；推理轨迹会增加输出token，导致计算需求上升。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹在推理阶段的引入能提升法律文档翻译质量，但需考虑计算成本与质量的权衡；RLVR的有效性部分归因于推理能力的诱导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been established as a viable paradigm for the post-training of Large Language Models (LLMs), including downstream tasks, such as Neural Machine Translation (NMT). With the latest research indicating that RLVR could be the preferred training method for translating legal documents due to the induced reasoning capabilities, it raises the question whether it is really attributed to the reasoning or more generally to the training paradigm. We investigate the importance of including the model's reasoning trace in the generated responses during both training and inference by systematically omitting it from one of the phases. Our experiments show that including the reasoning, specifically during inference, has a positive effect on the overall translation quality. Furthermore, we recognise that the reasoning leads to an increase in output tokens, hence we study the cost-quality tradeoff between the increased computational demands and the improved translation quality.

</details>


### [38] [Selection Shapes the Boundary: A Preregistered Replication of Monotonicity and Label Agreement in Unselected NLI Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19231)
*Haram Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文复现了先前关于非上升单调性算子在NLI中导致标签一致性降低的发现，但在未经选择的SNLI和MultiNLI开发集上未能复现，反而发现非上升单调性项目的一致性略高，且效应量远小于关注阈值，得出结论：先前的负面边界可能是低一致性选择条件下的产物，而非总体层面属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究依赖于按分歧水平选择的重新标注资源，发现非上升单调性算子在ChaosNLI中导致标签一致性降低。本文旨在验证这一发现是否为总体层面属性，还是仅在低一致性选择条件下出现。

Method: 预注册复现实验，在SNLI和MultiNLI开发集（未经选择的总体）上使用相同的算子标记器和四级有序一致性结果，进行七组对比，计算Cliff's delta，并设定最小关注效应量为0.10。同时进行稳健性检查：模拟标记器误分类和人工重新标记审计。

Result: 所有七组对比均返回正的Cliff's delta（非上升单调性项目一致性略高），唯一显著的确证性对比符号与预注册相反，所有效应量远低于0.10。稳健性检查支持测量有效性：模拟误分类会缩小效应而非制造效应，人工审计四类一致性达0.875。

Conclusion: 先前的负面边界很可能是低一致性选择条件下的结构，而非总体层面属性。基于选定重新标注资源的HLV结构主张应明确说明其选择条件。

Abstract: Prior work on human label variation (HLV) in natural language inference (NLI) has often relied on re-annotation resources that select items by disagreement level. An earlier study (arXiv:2607.15870) found that hypotheses containing non-upward monotonicity operators showed lower label agreement in ChaosNLI (Cliff's delta = -0.284), which is restricted to items whose majority label carries exactly three of five votes. We preregistered a replication of this boundary in the unselected populations that ChaosNLI was drawn from: the SNLI and MultiNLI development sets, using the same operator tagger and a four-level ordinal agreement outcome. The registered prediction fails. All seven contrasts return a positive Cliff's delta (non-upward items agree slightly more, not less), the only significant confirmatory contrast has the opposite sign to the registration, and every effect is far below our smallest effect size of interest (0.10). Robustness checks support the measurement: simulated tagger misclassification shrinks the effects rather than manufacturing them, and a manual re-tagging audit reaches four-class agreement of 0.875 on a fresh 200-item sample. We conclude that the earlier negative boundary is plausibly a structure conditional on low-agreement selection rather than a population-level property, and that HLV structure claims built on selected re-annotation resources should state their selection conditional explicitly.

</details>


### [39] [MeetingToM: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Theory-of-Mind Reasoning in Multi-Party Meetings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19235)
*Ziyi Wang,Yuhang Wu,Dongxu Piao,Xingyu Liu,Tianhui Zhou,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MeetingToM 基准，旨在评估多模态大语言模型在自然多方会议中的心理理论推理能力，重点关注伪共识等会议特有的社会现象，分层次考察主体级心理状态预测、二元级受话人理解和群体级共识推理。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 ToM 基准主要聚焦于视频接地的显性、可外部验证信号的问答，对潜在社会状态和群体动态覆盖不足，难以评估模型在真实多方会议中整合非言语线索、推断隐性态度及区分真实共识与伪共识的能力。

Method: 构建 MeetingToM 基准，按社会粒度分为三级：（i）主体级心理状态预测，（ii）二元级受话人理解，（iii）群体级共识推理，并提供统一评估协议；选取代表性 MLLM 进行系统分析。

Result: 实验揭示当前 MLLM 在整合非言语线索、推断隐性态度、区分真实共识与伪共识等方面存在持续局限，凸显了会议接地 ToM 推理的关键挑战。

Conclusion: MeetingToM 为推进多模态模型在会议场景下的心理理论推理提供了重要测试床，未来工作需重点解决多模态线索融合、隐性社会状态建模及群体动态推理等难题。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer other's beliefs, intentions, and states of knowledge, is central to social interaction, yet remains challenging for current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), especially in multi-party meetings where cues are distributed across speech and behavior. Existing multimodal ToM benchmarks mainly focus on video-grounded question answering over overt, externally verifiable signals, and provide limited coverage of latent social states and group dynamics. We introduce MeetingToM, a benchmark for complex social behavior reasoning in naturalistic multi-party meetings. MeetingToM targets meeting-specific phenomena such as \textbf{pseudo-consensus}, where apparent agreement masks private dissent under social pressure. The benchmark is hierarchically organized to evaluate ToM at increasing levels of social granularity, including (i) subject-level mental state prediction, (ii) dyadic-level addressee understanding, and (iii) group-level consensus reasoning. We provide a unified evaluation protocol and conduct systematic analyses of representative MLLMs, revealing persistent limitations in integrating non-verbal cues, inferring hidden attitudes, and distinguishing genuine consensus from pseudo-consensus. Our results highlight key challenges and establish MeetingToM as a testbed for advancing meeting-grounded ToM in multimodal models.

</details>


### [40] [Inference-Time Steering for Cross-Lingual Factual Consistency in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19243)
*Alexander Manev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型（LLM）在跨语言事实一致性方面的偏见问题，并评估了四种推理时干预策略。实验表明，人设提示（persona prompting）在效果、安全性和域外泛化上表现最佳，而CAA虽能显著改变基准表现但对配置敏感且可能导致知识退化，DPO适配器提供永久但较窄且泛化较差的增益。结论指出跨语言不一致至少部分是一个选择问题，简单的上下文干预可能优于更侵入式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽展现多语言流利度，但内部知识表征严重偏向高资源语言，导致跨语言事实不一致：仅因提示语言不同，模型会改变经验答案分布。作者旨在推理阶段缓解此偏见，使英语提示下的模型能像用目标语言（德语、西班牙语、保加利亚语）查询时那样回答。

Method: 评估四种干预策略：零样本上下文引导（人设提示）、对比激活加法（CAA）的内部表征操纵、基于直接偏好优化（DPO）的轻量级权重修改（在基准衍生事实数据和概念泛化数据上训练）。构建多语言事实数据集和新颖泛化基准（包含文化根植查询）以评估对齐情况。实验在Gemma 3 12B Instruct上进行。

Result: 人设提示为整体最强干预，平衡了功效、安全性和域外泛化。CAA在不一致基准上产生剧烈变化，但对配置敏感且有知识退化风险。基于DPO的适配器提供永久但较窄且可迁移性较差的增益。

Conclusion: 跨语言不一致至少部分是一个选择问题，简单的上下文干预（如人设提示）可能比更侵入式方法更能实现稳健、可迁移的对齐。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multilingual fluency, their internal knowledge representations remain disproportionately biased toward high-resource languages. This leads to cross-lingual factual inconsistency, where they shift their empirical answer distributions based solely on the prompt language. We investigate whether these biases can be mitigated at inference time, forcing an English-prompted model to answer as if it were queried in target languages (German, Spanish, Bulgarian), and evaluate four intervention strategies: zero-shot contextual steering (persona prompting), internal representation manipulation via Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), and lightweight weight modification via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trained on benchmark-derived factual data as well as conceptual generalization data. To assess alignment, we curate a multilingual factual dataset alongside a novel generalization benchmark comprising culturally rooted queries to determine whether factual interventions transfer to broader target-centric preferences. Experiments on Gemma 3 12B Instruct reveal persona prompting to be the strongest overall intervention, balancing efficacy, safety, and out-of-domain generalization. While CAA yields sharp inconsistency benchmark shifts, it is configuration-sensitive and risks knowledge degradation. DPO-based adapters offer permanent, yet narrower and less transferable gains. These findings suggest that cross-lingual inconsistency is at least partly a selection problem, and that simple contextual interventions may outperform more invasive methods for robust, transferable alignment.

</details>


### [41] [Prompt Design at Scale: How Format, Instruction Count, and Context Length Shape Instruction Adherence and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19257)
*Netanel Eliav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过两项大规模控制实验，系统评估了提示格式、指令数量和上下文长度对大语言模型指令遵循、召回准确性及拒答行为的影响。实验基于合成语料库“Book of Veyra”，跨五个模型测试。核心发现：指令遵循率在规则数N=80时普遍归零；提示位置（系统提示vs用户轮次）影响显著且模型特异；无模型稳定受益于Markdown格式。长上下文召回在64-128k tokens后急剧下降且格式依赖性强，最高准确率差距达48个百分点；捏造事实未发生，谄媚率极低；临近上下文上限时模型主要表现为拒答（高达79-90%），而非捏造或谄媚。Token开销进一步改变最优格式选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践者在三个提示设计决策上缺乏受控证据：1) 指令与上下文的格式化方式（Markdown、纯文本、散文、表格）；2) 系统提示可承载多少条并发指令才导致合规性下降；3) 模型能容纳多少上下文才导致召回与诚实度下降。

Method: 构建无污染合成语料库“Book of Veyra”（8,780个唯一命名实体，固定种子确定性再生），跨五个模型开展两项全因子交叉实验。实验1：960次调用/模型，测量规则数N从10增至160时的指令遵循衰减，交叉四种格式与系统提示/用户轮次放置位置。实验2：5,520次调用/模型，测量2k至512k token上下文阶梯上的召回准确率、虚假前提谄媚率、缺失事实捏造率，同样交叉四种格式。

Result: 实验1：完美响应率在N=80时对所有模型、格式、位置均归零。位置效应在N=160时至少与格式效应相当，但方向具有模型特异性。无模型显示稳定的Markdown优势；一款35B模型反而偏好纯文本。实验2：召回准确率在64-128k tokens前维持天花板水平，随后急剧下降且强依赖格式：单一模型在128k tokens处准确率差距达48个百分点。捏造事实从未发生（0/5,760探测），谄媚率可忽略（≤8.3%）。临近各模型上下文上限时，直接拒答率从0%飙升至79-90%，区别于谄媚或捏造。预注册的格式排序均不成立，Token开销（较纯文本+22%至+37%）进一步改变最优格式选择。

Conclusion: 提示格式、指令数量、上下文长度对模型行为有显著交互影响，且高度模型特异。Markdown无普遍优势，纯文本在某些大模型上更优。长上下文召回衰退由格式调节，拒答而非捏造是上下文饱和的主要失效模式。发布完整实验框架、语料生成器及原始结果（VeyraBench）。

Abstract: Practitioners make three prompt-design decisions with almost no controlled evidence behind them: how to format instructions and context (markdown, plain text, prose, or tabular), how many simultaneous instructions a system prompt can carry before compliance degrades, and how much context a model can hold before recall and honesty degrade. We report two controlled experiments crossing all three factors on one held, contamination-free synthetic corpus (the "Book of Veyra," 8,780 uniquely-named entities, deterministically regenerable from a fixed seed), evaluated across five models.
  Experiment 1 (960 calls/model) measures instruction-following decay as rule count N grows from 10 to 160, crossed with four formats and system-prompt vs. user-turn placement. Perfect-response rate collapses to zero by N=80 for every model, format, and placement. Placement produces effects at least as large as format at N=160 in most models, but the direction is model-specific. No model shows a reliable markdown advantage; one 35B model favors plain text instead.
  Experiment 2 (5,520 calls/model) measures recall accuracy, false-premise sycophancy, and absent-fact fabrication across a 2k-to-512k-token context ladder in the same four formats. Recall stays near ceiling through 64-128k tokens, then degrades sharply and format-dependently: one model's accuracy spread reaches 48 points at 128k tokens. Fabrication never occurs (0/5,760 probes), and sycophancy stays negligible (<=8.3%). What rises sharply near each model's context ceiling is outright refusal to answer (0% to 79-90%), distinct from sycophancy or fabrication. Neither pre-registered format ordering holds, and token overhead (+22% to +37% over plain text) further changes which format is preferable where accuracy spread is genuine.
  We release the full harness, corpus generator, and raw results (VeyraBench): https://github.com/iNetanel/veyrabench

</details>


### [42] [Two-Level Meta-Rubrics for Evaluating Open-Ended Generation: GAMUT, a Benchmark for Factual Completeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19322)
*Xilun Chen,Zhaleh Feizollahi,Ross Goodwin,Seungwhan Moon,Scott Yih,Pinar Donmez,Babak Damavandi,Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出双层元评分框架 Gamut，填补长文本生成事实完整性评估的空白，构建含 1,813 个多领域问题的基准，测试 14 个模型显示基准具挑战性（最优 58.7%）、高区分度且对裁判选择鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实性评估聚焦精确度（主张是否正确），忽略事实完整性。完整性评估需枚举完整答案应含的所有事实，但这些事实常形成开放集合覆盖、有序过程及事实间关系，非扁平布尔检查所能捕捉。

Method: 设计双层元评分表示：结构化元评分捕捉所需内容的组织与重要性，再机械编译为扁平的二值、机器可评分评分项，由 LLM 裁判打分。基于真实可穿戴图像跨 10 个领域构建 1,813 个问题，配专家验证的证据支持评分项，并发布纯文本变体。

Result: 评测 14 个前沿与开源模型，Gemini 3.1 Pro 以 58.7% 取得最高分，基准表现出真实挑战性、高区分度，且对裁判模型选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Gamut 通过双层元评分框架有效量化长文本生成的事实完整性，提供模态无关、可靠且具区分度的基准，推动事实性评估从精确度向完整性延伸。

Abstract: Evaluating the factuality of long-form generations has focused predominantly on precision, measuring whether the claims a model makes are correct. The dominant decompose-search-verify pipeline catches incorrect claims well but says little about whether a response contains all the information it should. Measuring factual completeness, the missing half of factuality, is harder: it requires enumerating the full set of facts a complete answer should contain, and these facts rarely form a flat list. They often involve open-ended sets where coverage is what matters, ordered processes, and relationships among facts that a list of independent boolean checks fails to capture. We introduce a two-level meta-rubric framework for evaluating open-ended generation, and instantiate it as Gamut (Grounded Assessment of Multimodal Factuality), a benchmark for factual completeness in long-form generation. The framework rests on a two-level rubric representation: a structured meta-rubric captures the organization and importance of the required content, which is then mechanically compiled into a flat checklist of binary, machine-gradable rubrics that an LLM judge scores reliably. We construct 1,813 questions grounded in real wearable imagery across 10 diverse domains, each paired with an evidence-backed rubric verified by expert human annotators. Because the framework is modality-agnostic, we also release a text-only variant. Evaluating 14 frontier and open-weight models, we find the benchmark genuinely challenging (best score 58.7% from Gemini 3.1 Pro), highly discriminative, and robust to the choice of judge.

</details>


### [43] [Selective State-Space Adaptation and Retrieval for Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19326)
*Atahan Dokme,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一系列适配器（MaLoRA 和 MaRA），在标记级和上下文级引入选择性状态空间循环，显著提升冻结大语言模型在多跳推理任务上的表现，在多个主干模型和基准上全面超越 LoRA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适应（LoRA）提供静态的任务级更新，无法显式表示标记级或实例级的状态变化，缺乏动态的、输入相关的自适应能力。

Method: 1. MaLoRA（Mamba 调制低秩适应）：在标记级使适配器的缩放因子成为具有跨标记循环状态的动态输入相关函数。2. MaRA（Mamba 检索适配器）：在上下文级跟踪跨段状态并选择与查询最相关的片段，再由调制语言模型生成答案。

Result: 在三个冻结主干（Qwen-2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B）和两个推理基准（MuSiQue、2WikiMultihopQA）上，该系列方法在 3×2 网格的每个单元格上均提升推理准确性，平均 +6.8 F1（+10.5% 相对提升），最难单元格高达 +9.3 F1（+18.2% 相对提升）；标记级增益还延伸至 RULER QA-2 长文本压力测试。

Conclusion: 通过在互补粒度引入选择性状态空间循环，该适配器家族有效增强了冻结大语言模型的推理能力，实现了细粒度的动态自适应。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation introduces a static learned update applied identically to every input. The update provides task-level adaptation but does not explicitly represent token-level or instance-level state variation. A family of adapters is proposed that introduces selective state-space recurrence at two complementary granularities. At the token level, \textbf{MaLoRA} (Mamba-modulated low-rank adaptation) makes the adapter's scaling factor a dynamic input-dependent function with recurrent state across tokens, in contrast to the stateless modulators of prior work. At the context level, \textbf{MaRA} (Mamba Retrieval Adapter) tracks cross-segment state and selects the segments most relevant to the query, before the modulated language model generates its answer. Across three frozen backbones (Qwen-2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B) and two reasoning benchmarks (MuSiQue, 2WikiMultihopQA), the family improves reasoning accuracy on every cell of the $3{\times}2$ grid, by $+6.8$ F1 ($+10.5\%$ relative) on average and up to $+9.3$ F1 ($+18.2\%$ relative) on the hardest cell over the LoRA baseline, and the token-level gains carry to RULER QA-2 under length stress.

</details>


### [44] [Copy Less, Ground More: Overcoming Repetitive Copying in Long-Context Reasoning via Evidence-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19345)
*Lizhe Fang,Weizhou Shen,Tianyi Tang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现长上下文大语言模型在推理时存在“重复复制”失败模式，即模型大量复制输入文本而非真正推理。根因是缺乏锚定：模型无差别复制提示词，未能聚焦关键证据。为此提出 GEAR（基于证据感知的奖励），通过锚定奖励和干扰惩罚改进强化学习，并构建自动化证据标注流水线。实验显示 GEAR 在多规模模型和基准上平均提升 4.6 分，长上下文增益更大，同时减少重复复制和思维长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型在复杂推理任务中表现强劲，但发现一个关键失败模式：模型在推理轨迹中大量复制输入文本（重复复制），而非进行有效推理。这种行为随上下文长度增加而加剧，且模型无差别复制提示词，未能聚焦任务相关的关键证据，导致回答错误。因此需要一种方法引导模型准确锚定关键证据、抑制无关干扰。

Method: 提出 GEAR（Grounding Evidence-Aware Reward）奖励塑形方法：在基于准确率的奖励基础上，增加两个信号——锚定奖励（鼓励推理与关键证据的重叠）和干扰惩罚（惩罚推理与无关上下文的重叠）。同时开发自动化流水线，从任意文档构建带证据标注的训练数据，以便在自然语言数据上应用 GEAR。

Result: 在多种模型规模和基准上验证 GEAR，相较于仅使用准确率奖励的标准 RL，平均性能提升高达 +4.6 分，且在更长上下文中增益更大。同时显著减少重复复制行为和思维长度，表明模型更高效地聚焦关键证据进行推理。

Conclusion: 即使长上下文评估从简单检索转向复杂推理，准确锚定相关证据仍是不可或缺的核心能力，且仍有巨大改进空间。GEAR 通过显式奖励锚定行为，有效缓解了重复复制问题，提升了长上下文推理的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Large language models that generate step-by-step reasoning traces have achieved strong performance on complex tasks, and extending them to long-context settings has emerged as an important frontier. However, we identify a critical failure mode in this regime: \emph{repetitive copying}, where models extensively copy text from the input into their reasoning traces rather than productively solving the problem. We show that this behavior is pervasive across frontier long-context LLMs and intensifies with context length. By separating each prompt into task-relevant key evidence and irrelevant distractor context, we further show that the root cause is insufficient grounding: models copy from the prompt indiscriminately, and those that fail to focus on key evidence are far more likely to answer incorrectly. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose GEAR (Grounding Evidence-Aware Reward), a reward shaping method that augments the accuracy signal with a grounding reward for overlap with key evidence and a distractor penalty for overlap with irrelevant context. To enable GEAR on natural-language data, we develop an automated pipeline that constructs evidence-annotated training data from arbitrary documents. We validate GEAR across multiple model scales and benchmarks, showing consistent improvements of up to +4.6 average points over standard RL with accuracy-based rewards, with larger gains at longer contexts, while also reducing repetitive copying and thinking length. Our findings suggest that, even as long-context evaluation shifts from simple retrieval toward complex reasoning, accurate grounding in relevant evidence remains an indispensable capability with substantial room for improvement.

</details>


### [45] [A Classifier That Teaches Itself: Self-Improving, Frozen-gate Training (SIFT) for Dynamic Document Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18358)
*Bogdan Raduta,Horia Velicu,Alexandru Preda,Serban Chiricescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SIFT是一种自改进的动态文档分类服务，通过廉价CPU管道（SPLADE+LightGBM）配合LLM裁判回环实现零前置标注成本自动化，并引入双重冻结门控机制（关键标签F1回归检查+从不参训的黄金回归集）保证自主重训练安全，将边际标注成本压至趋近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级文档分类的核心阻碍非模型架构，而是高昂的前置标注项目成本与机构对模型自主重训练可能导致静默回归的恐惧。现有方案难以同时解决标注瓶颈与重训练安全性。

Method: 1) 架构：SPLADE稀疏编码器+LightGBM分类头组成的CPU束缚廉价管道服务推理；2) 自我喂养循环：仅将低置信度少数样本升级至LLM裁判，裁判裁决回写标注语料，昂贵模型持续教导廉价模型；3) 声明式接入：新文档族仅需标签空间、锚点短语、裁判术语表，无需标注项目；4) 冻结门控晋升：双重否决机制——关键标签F1回归检查与从不参训的冻结黄金回归集，任一触发即阻止模型推广。

Result: 多领域部署表明：升级率随语料增长持续下降，语料库从生产流量自然积累，准确率呈复利增长；实现‘无人值守月度重训练’从鲁莽变常规；边际标注成本趋近零。

Conclusion: SIFT通过自改进语料循环与冻结门控安全机制，彻底解决了企业文档分类的标注瓶颈与自主重训练信任危机，提供了一种经济可行、安全可控的生产级动态分类服务范式。

Abstract: Document classification is a solved problem in the laboratory and an unsolved one in the enterprise. The blocker is rarely model architecture; it is the labeling project that must precede a model and the institutional fear of letting a model retrain itself once one exists. We present SIFT (Self-Improving, Frozen-gate Training), a dynamic classifier service, which attacks both. SIFT serves classification from a deliberately cheap, CPU-bound pipeline, a SPLADE sparse encoder feeding a LightGBM head, and escalates only the low-confidence minority of pages to an LLM judge. The judge's verdicts are written back into a labeled corpus, so the expensive model continuously teaches the cheap one: the escalation rate falls, the corpus grows from production traffic rather than from an up-front annotation effort, and accuracy compounds with use. Onboarding a new document family requires only a declarative bundle, label space, anchor phrases, and a judge glossary, not a labeling project. The harder problem is safety: an autonomously retraining classifier can silently regress. SIFT resolves this with a two-part promote gate, a critical-label F1 regression check plus a frozen golden regression set the model is never trained on, either of which vetoes promotion. This turns "retrain monthly without a human" from reckless into routine. We describe the architecture, the self-feeding corpus loop, the frozen-gate promotion mechanism, and an illustrative multi-domain deployment, and we discuss the economics of a classifier whose marginal labeling cost trends toward zero.

</details>

*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC，一种基于联邦学习的胎儿超声胼胝体定位框架，结合冻结的DINOv2骨干网络、轻量级YOLO检测头和LoRA适配模块，在多中心数据上实现高性能、低通信开销的隐私保护定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿超声中胼胝体的准确定位对神经发育异常的早期筛查至关重要，但受限于超声成像的低对比度、斑点噪声及解剖变异大等固有缺陷，且多中心临床数据因隐私法规难以共享，现有集中式训练难以部署。

Method: 设计FedCC联邦学习框架：采用冻结的DINOv2作为骨干网络，接轻量级YOLO检测头；引入低秩适配（LoRA）模块仅优化少量参数；客户端本地训练后通过FedAvg聚合LoRA参数，实现参数高效的模型适应与隐私保护。

Result: 在包含3个临床中心、58例孕妇、10,970帧超声图像的多中心数据集上，FedAvg策略下DINOv2+LoRA组合达到平均mAP@50 0.857、F1-score 0.803，优于全量微调与编码器冻结基线；可训练参数量从24.4M降至2.9M，通信开销降低约8.5倍。

Conclusion: FedCC展示了在资源受限、多中心临床环境中部署可扩展、隐私保护的胎儿神经超声AI系统的可行性，为联邦学习在医学影像分析中的实际落地提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

</details>


### [47] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合参数重要性与逐层功能等价性的低秩近似目标，并设计了高效的动态压缩率分配算法，实验表明其在高压缩率下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数量大导致内存需求高，限制在资源受限环境部署；现有SVD压缩方法要么仅考虑参数重要性，要么仅考虑逐层功能等价性，且压缩率分配多为均匀或依赖昂贵启发式搜索。

Method: 将参数重要性与逐层功能等价性融合为单一目标函数进行低秩近似，并提出计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法。

Result: 实验结果显示该方法性能持平或显著优于先前最先进方法，尤其在高压缩比下表现更优。

Conclusion: 结合多视角目标与高效动态压缩率分配能有效提升神经网络压缩质量，为大模型在资源受限设备上部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

</details>


### [48] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E-SpecFormer，一种用于端到端自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别的边缘频谱监测Transformer，引入无Softmax和LayerNorm的LiTAN注意力机制，在极低参数量(<10k)和高速度(92μs/帧)下超越现有边缘模型，实现IoT设备上的实时频谱智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对射频任务的深度学习模型在边缘设备部署时面临计算复杂度高、参数量大、推理延迟高的挑战，难以满足IoT设备实时频谱监测的需求，需要一种轻量级、高效率的端到端解决方案。

Method: 提出E-SpecFormer架构，核心创新是LiTAN（线性Tanh注意力网络），一种无Softmax和LayerNorm的注意力机制，降低计算复杂度同时提升精度；提供Nano/Small/Medium/Large四种可扩展变体适应不同硬件约束；采用FPGA/CPU协同执行优化推理速度。

Result: 在RadioML2018调制识别数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB下达到86.5%平均准确率；在硬件木马隐蔽信道数据集上达到94.2%准确率；参数量<10k，FPGA/CPU协同执行速度达92μs/帧，显著优于现有最先进边缘模型且成本更低。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer确立了一种边缘高效的实时频谱智能解决方案，通过创新的LiTAN注意力机制和可扩展架构设计，在极低资源消耗下实现了最优性能，为IoT设备上的自动调制识别和隐蔽信道检测提供了可行的部署路径。

Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

</details>


### [49] [Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Runtime-Tunable Transit Signal Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18286)
*Philip-Roman Adam,Stefanie Schmidtner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于偏好条件的交通信号优先控制器，可在运行时通过偏好参数权衡公交优先与整体交通延误，无需重新训练即可适应不同时段和干扰条件下的机构优先级变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的交通信号优先（TSP）方法通常优化固定奖励或固定标量化，缺乏运行时灵活性，难以适应不同时段或干扰条件下机构优先级的变化。

Method: 提出偏好条件控制器 π(a|s,w)，在满足最小/最大绿灯时间和相位转换可行性约束下选择信号相位，通过偏好参数 w 在运行时调整公交优先权重与整体延误的权衡。基于 IntersectionZoo 实现约束信号控制/TSP 包装器，并扩展场景生成，引入公交流量增强和基于时刻表的公交插入，解决训练中公交优先事件稀疏的问题。

Result: 实验表明，单一条件策略在运行时偏好下形成平滑的经验权衡前沿，优于定时控制、基于规则的 TSP 叠加和固定权重 PPO 专家策略，同时保持约束可行性。尾部延误诊断显示，中等偏好设置下非公交外部性有限，但高公交优先权重下可能显著增加。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了一个灵活的、可运行时调整的 TSP 控制器，在保持约束满足的同时提供了跨偏好的平滑性能权衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Transit signal priority (TSP) requires balancing competing objectives: reducing bus delay while limiting adverse impacts on non-bus traffic and avoiding extreme waits for a subset of vehicles. Existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches to TSP typically encode transit-aware features (e.g., occupancy and schedule deviation) but optimize a fixed reward or fixed scalarization, which limits operational flexibility when agency priorities change across time-of-day or disruption conditions. We present a preference-conditioned TSP controller, $π(a \mid s,w)$, that selects the next signal phase under minimum/maximum green and transition-feasibility constraints and can be tuned at runtime via a preference parameter $w$ to trade off bus-priority emphasis against overall traffic delay without retraining. We implement this on top of IntersectionZoo by introducing a constrained signal-control/TSP wrapper, and we extend scenario generation with bus-prevalence augmentation and timetable-based bus insertion to address sparse transit-priority events during training. Experiments against fixed-time control, a rule-based TSP overlay, and fixed-weight PPO specialists show that a single learned conditioned policy spans a smooth empirical trade-off frontier across runtime preferences, outperforms fixed-time and rule-based baselines, and maintains constraint feasibility, while tail-delay diagnostics reveal that non-bus externalities remain limited for moderate preference settings but can increase substantially under high bus-priority weights. The source code of this work is available at https://github.com/urbanAIthi/morl-tsp.

</details>


### [50] [BearingNAS: Obtaining In-Sensor Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems for Bearings Using a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18287)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Rodolfo Zunino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BearingNAS 是一个面向极端微预算（4-8 KiB RAM，16-32 KiB Flash）的硬件感知神经架构搜索框架，将轴承故障诊断智能直接部署到传感器芯片内部。通过轻量级无导数搜索策略和衰减核增长搜索空间，在笔记本 CPU 上不到一小时完成搜索，在 ISPU 上达到 99.50% 诊断准确率，验证了传感器内机器学习的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决工业轴承故障诊断中将模型部署到资源极度受限的传感器芯片（ISPU/MCU）的难题：现有 NAS 方法依赖昂贵 GPU 且搜索空间参数易爆炸，难以满足 4-8 KiB RAM 与 16-32 KiB Flash 的严苛约束，且缺乏面向传感器内计算的硬件感知搜索框架。

Method: 提出 BearingNAS 框架：1) 将搜索建模为受约束优化问题，目标为极端微预算；2) 设计轻量级无导数搜索策略，消除对离散 GPU 的依赖；3) 构建单一数据流搜索空间，引入衰减核增长公式动态控制核大小与数量，防止参数爆炸；4) 面向 STMicroelectronics 两款商用 MCU 与 LSM6DSO16IS ISPU 进行硬件感知搜索。

Result: 在 CWRU 轴承基准上实验：搜索全程在笔记本 CPU 运行，<1 小时收敛；针对 ISPU 的最佳架构达到 99.50% 诊断准确率，同时满足 4-8 KiB RAM 与 16-32 KiB Flash 约束，验证了在传感器封装内直接运行高精度故障诊断模型的可行性。

Conclusion: BearingNAS 证明了通过硬件感知神经架构搜索，可在极端资源受限的传感器芯片上部署高精度轴承故障诊断模型，实现低成本、生产规模的传感器内智能诊断，为工业物联网边缘智能提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces BearingNAS, a Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search (HW-NAS) framework designed to shift the intelligence directly onto the sensor die via in-sensor processing. BearingNAS frames the search as a constrained optimization problem targeting extreme micro-budgets (4 to 8 kiB of RAM and 16 to 32 kiB of Flash). To eliminate the reliance on expensive discrete GPUs, we propose a lightweight, derivative-free search strategy paired with a single data-flow search space that leverages a decaying kernel growth formulation to prevent parameter explosion. We evaluate our framework on the Case Western Reserve University (CWRU) bearing benchmark, optimizing architectures for three STMicroelectronics targets: two commodity microcontrollers and the LSM6DSO16IS Intelligent Sensor Processing Unit (ISPU). Running entirely on a laptop CPU, the search converges in less than an hour. The resulting best in-sensor architecture achieves a highly competitive diagnostic accuracy of 99.50\% on the ISPU. These results demonstrate the viability of shifting the machine learning workload inside the sensor package, enabling low-cost, production-scale bearing fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [51] [Multi-Timescale Latent-Action DRL for Joint Optimization in Edge-Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18288)
*Vo Phi Son,Van-Dinh Nguyen,Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Trinh Van Chien,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分层边缘-云计算系统中的负载不均衡导致的高延迟问题，提出双时间尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），联合优化服务部署、计算委派、任务卸载和功率控制，利用变分自编码器压缩高维动作空间，实现近似最优性能，平均端到端延迟降低20.8%，资源利用率提升13%，收敛速度提升约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层边缘-云计算系统中，边缘层与云层间的负载不均衡在动态任务到达和异构资源条件下导致严重的排队时延和资源利用率低下，现有方法难以联合优化离散的服务部署/用户关联决策与连续的功率控制/任务卸载决策，形成混合整数非凸NP-hard问题。

Method: 利用决策动态的固有差异，将原问题分解为长期系统配置（服务部署、用户关联、计算委派）和短期资源分配（任务卸载、发射功率）两个子问题。提出双时间尺度多层深度强化学习框架（2T-MDRL-LA），引入基于变分自编码器的潜在动作表示，压缩高维组合动作空间，实现离散与连续变量的联合高效优化。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该框架能有效适应动态网络条件，性能接近分支定界最优解。相比无计算委派方案，平均端到端时延降低高达20.8%，资源利用率提升13%；收敛速度比传统近端策略优化（PPO）快约50%。

Conclusion: 通过双时间尺度分解与潜在动作空间压缩，2T-MDRL-LA框架有效解决了分层边缘-云系统中的联合服务部署、计算委派与功率控制难题，在时延、资源利用率和收敛速度上均显著优于基准算法，为动态异构边缘云环境下的实时资源编排提供了可扩展的智能解决方案。

Abstract: Load imbalance across edge and cloud layers degrades latency performance in hierarchical edge-cloud computing (HECC) systems under dynamic task arrivals and heterogeneous resources, leading to severe queuing delays and inefficient resource utilization. To address this challenge, we study a joint service placement, computational delegation, and power control (JSCP) problem to minimize the average end-to-end (e2e) latency. The resulting JSCP problem is a mixed-integer nonconvex and NP-hard optimization problem due to the strong coupling between discrete and continuous variables. To enable tractable optimization and stable system adaptation, we exploit the inherent difference in decision dynamics and decompose the problem into long-term system configuration and short-term resource allocation subproblems. Based on this formulation, we propose a two-timescale multi-layer deep reinforcement learning framework with a latent action space (2T-MDRL-LA) to jointly optimize service placement, user association, computational delegation, task offloading, and user transmit power. A latent action representation based on a variational autoencoder is introduced to efficiently compress the high-dimensional combinatorial action space. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts to dynamic network conditions and achieves near-optimal performance compared to branch-and-bound solutions. It achieves up to a 20.8% reduction in average e2e latency and a 13% improvement in resource utilization over the scheme without the computational delegation, while converging approximately 50% faster than conventional proximal policy optimization.

</details>


### [52] [Exposure-Based Reinforcement Learning to Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18689)
*Harrie Oosterhuis,Rolf Jagerman,Zhen Qin,Xuanhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于曝光分布的强化学习排序新方法，通过方差缩减和GPU计算避免了复杂的自定义梯度，实现了更快收敛、更高性能且易于集成自动微分框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习排序方法依赖复杂的自定义梯度计算，实现困难且与自动微分框架冲突，导致计算效率低、稳定性差，难以被从业者广泛采用。

Method: 提出基于文档曝光分布的抽象层，将梯度估计置于曝光分布之后，结合基线校正和部分边缘化实现方差缩减，利用GPU并行计算，使损失函数只需作为曝光的可微函数实现即可通过自动微分优化。

Result: 实验显示新方法收敛显著更快，排序性能更高，且在GPU上无额外计算时间成本；现有自定义梯度方法在长周期训练中存在严重稳定性问题，而新方法从未出现。

Conclusion: 通过引入曝光分布抽象和方差缩减技术，大幅提升了强化学习排序的有效性、效率和易用性，使其能无缝集成现代深度学习框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) methods for learning-to-rank (LTR) can optimize (almost) any ranking goal, e.g., from precision or discounted cumulative gain to fairness-of-exposure or ranking distillation. However, standard RL is ineffective and computationally costly due to the enormous action space in LTR settings. Existing methods reach computational efficiency through custom gradient computation algorithms, but they are very complex to implement and often clash with auto-differentiation. Consequently, existing RL for LTR is not attractive to many practitioners. We reconsider RL for LTR while actively avoiding reliance on custom gradients. Contrary to the existing approaches, we focus on variance reduction and GPU computation. In doing so, we discover that high sample-efficiency can be reached through baseline corrections and partial marginalization. Furthermore, we propose an abstraction that places gradient estimation behind a document-exposure distribution, this enables seamless plug-and-play integration with auto-differentiation. Thereby, one only has to implement a loss as a differentiable function of exposure and RL for LTR can optimize it using auto-differentiation. Our experimental results reveal that our new exposure-based RL for LTR approach converges considerably faster and at significantly higher ranking performance than existing custom gradients, with no additional costs in computation time when using GPUs. In contrast, existing custom gradients result in severe stability issues when converging over many epochs, which never occur for our methods. Thus, we considerably improve RL for LTR methodology by increasing its effectiveness, efficiency, and ease of application.

</details>


### [53] [Towards Principled Continual Anomaly Detection: A Systematic Framework and Benchmark Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18289)
*Kamil Faber,Mateusz Smendowski,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个系统框架，用于从现有表格异常检测数据集构建可复现的持续异常检测基准场景，解决了任务边界模糊、任务划分随意导致的不可学、冗余或过度迁移任务问题，并从三个大规模网络安全数据集生成了五个基准场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续异常检测基准高度依赖任务定义、过滤、排序和验证方式；表格领域任务边界通常不明确，随意划分会产生不可学、冗余或过度迁移的任务，掩盖真实的持续学习行为，因此需要系统框架来设计可复现的基准场景。

Method: 提出系统框架，包括发现候选任务、过滤不合适任务、推导原则性排序以暴露多样化动态，从而从现有表格异常检测数据集构建可复现的基准场景。

Result: 从三个大规模网络安全异常检测数据集生成了五个基准就绪场景，涵盖单数据集和多数据集的持续异常检测设置。

Conclusion: 该框架能系统性地构建可复现的持续异常检测基准，揭示多样化的持续学习动态，为表格领域的持续异常检测研究提供了坚实的评估基础。

Abstract: Continual anomaly detection (CAD) studies how models can adapt to evolving data distributions while retaining performance on previously observed regimes. CAD benchmarks, however, depend critically on how tasks are defined, filtered, ordered, and validated. In tabular domains, task boundaries are rarely given, and arbitrary splits can create unlearnable, redundant, or overly transferable tasks that obscure genuine continual-learning behavior. To this end, we introduce a systematic framework for reproducible benchmark scenario design from existing tabular anomaly-detection datasets. The framework discovers candidate tasks, filters unsuitable tasks, and derives principled orderings that expose diverse dynamics. The framework allows us to deliver five benchmark-ready scenarios from three large-scale cybersecurity anomaly detection datasets, yielding both single-dataset and multi-dataset CAD settings.

</details>

*Hoang-Thang Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SechKAN，一种基于双曲正割（sech）基函数的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，利用一维线性变换减少参数量，在函数拟合、PDE求解及图像分类任务上优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数量相当，运行时间优于其他KAN但略慢于MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN因在机器学习和科学计算中的有效性而受关注，提供了神经网络设计新范式。本文旨在利用sech函数的光滑钟形、局部响应和稳定梯度特性，并通过一维线性变换降低参数量，构建高效KAN架构。

Method: 提出SechKAN架构，采用双曲正割基函数，并引入一维线性变换以减少参数数量，使模型规模与多层感知机（MLP）相当。

Result: 在函数拟合、偏微分方程问题及MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100图像分类基准上，SechKAN性能优于MLP和其他KAN变体，参数量相近；运行时间优于其他KAN变体，但略长于MLP。

Conclusion: SechKAN作为一种新型KAN变体，结合sech基函数的优良数学性质与参数高效设计，在多种任务上实现了优越性能，同时保持了与MLP相当的模型规模，展示了其在科学计算和深度学习中的应用潜力。

Abstract: In recent years, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have attracted increasing attention due to their effectiveness in machine learning and scientific computing tasks, offering a new paradigm for neural network design. In this paper, we present SechKAN, a KAN architecture based on hyperbolic secant (sech) functions. The hyperbolic secant basis is used for its smooth bell-shaped form, localized responses, and stable gradients. We employ 1D linear transformations to reduce the number of parameters, allowing SechKAN to remain comparable to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) in model size. Experimental results indicate the effectiveness of SechKAN in function fitting, PDE problems, and image classification tasks on benchmark datasets, including MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100. SechKAN achieves superior performance compared to MLPs and other KAN variants while maintaining a similar number of parameters. However, its running time, while better than that of other KAN variants, is slightly longer than that of MLPs.

</details>


### [55] [Biological Amnesia in ICU Time-Series Prediction: A Drift-Adaptive Two-Stream Architecture with Temporal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19020)
*Fatema Ferdous Tamanna,K. M. Merajul Arefin,Md. Abdul Masud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自适应临床智能架构，用于ICU干预预测，通过结构解耦生理与治疗表示，仅在治疗流上进行参数更新，并引入归因驱动的时序RAG模块，实现可解释、可治理的模型自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持系统随治疗方案演变而静默退化，现有自适应方法将模型视为整体，无法区分稳定的患者生理特征与变化的机构实践，亟需一种能保留生理表示稳定性、仅针对漂移组件更新的自适应框架。

Method: 提出自适应临床智能架构，结构解耦生理与治疗表示，基于双重分布与准确性触发仅更新治疗流参数；自动化审计日志记录驱动自适应的治疗特征及其重要性变化；推理时采用归因驱动的时序RAG模块，将预测锚定于患者主导生理特征的时代匹配PubMed证据。实验使用84,792例MIMIC-IV住院记录（2008-2022），采用严格时间序列划分。

Result: 漂移完全定位于治疗流，验证了结构先验；选择性自适应在血管加压素和脓毒症休克判别与校准上优于静态源模型；完全重训练基线虽聚合判别略高，但漏报26例脓毒症休克，而本框架无反向漏报；框架保持了与自适应前源模型的检索一致性，而重训练基线大幅下降。

Conclusion: 将自适应约束于漂移组件并保留稳定生理表示，使临床AI能随实践演进而不扭曲患者生物学认知；该架构为高风险临床环境中可治理、可解释的自适应模型部署提供了范式。

Abstract: Background: Clinical decision support systems degrade silently as treatment protocols evolve, yet standard adaptation methods treat models as monolithic blocks, unable to distinguish stable patient physiology from shifting institutional practice. Methods: We propose an adaptive clinical intelligence architecture for ICU intervention prediction that structurally decouples physiological from treatment representations, confining parameter updates to the treatment stream upon a dual distributional and accuracy trigger. Automated audit logs record which treatment features drove each adaptation event and how their importance shifted. At inference, an attribution-driven Temporal RAG module grounds each prediction in patient-specific, era-matched PubMed evidence anchored to the patient's dominant physiological features. Experiments used 84,792 MIMIC-IV stays (2008-2022) under strict chronological split. Results: Drift localised entirely to the treatment stream, validating the structural prior. Selective adaptation improved vasopressor and septic shock discrimination and calibration over the static source model. A fully retrained baseline yielded marginally higher aggregate discrimination but missed 26 septic shock cases the framework correctly identified, with none in the reverse direction; retrieval consistency with the pre-adaptation source model was preserved by the framework but degraded substantially in the retrained baseline. Conclusions: Structurally constraining adaptation to drifting components while preserving stable physiological representations enables clinical AI to evolve with practice without distorting learned patient biology. This architecture offers a template for governable, interpretable deployment of adaptive models in high-stakes clinical environments.

</details>


### [56] [Dual-domain fused LSTM modeling for efficient time-dependent reliability analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18291)
*Yixin Zhang,Mingyang Li,Zichao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双域融合长短期记忆网络（DDF-LSTM）用于时变可靠度分析，将时不变变量嵌入初始隐藏状态，并设计改进损失函数强调最小响应敏感度，实现高效准确的失效概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理模型难以融合时不变随机变量并捕捉其与时变随机过程的复杂交互，限制了时变可靠度分析的精度与效率。

Method: 构建双域融合LSTM网络：时不变变量嵌入初始隐藏状态，全连接层将LSTM输出与时不变变量映射至输出空间；设计改进损失函数增强对最小响应的敏感度；训练后结合蒙特卡洛模拟高效估算时变失效概率。

Result: 通过四个算例验证，该方法在计算效率与预测精度上均优于现有方法，能有效捕捉随机变量、随机过程与极限状态函数时变行为间的依赖关系。

Conclusion: DDF-LSTM模型为时变可靠度分析提供了高效准确的代理建模工具，显著降低计算成本并提升失效概率估计精度，具有广泛的工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Time-dependent reliability analysis is crucial for ensuring the long-term safety and performance of engineering systems under uncertainties. However, traditional surrogate model methods often struggle to incorporate time-independent random variables and capture their complex interactions with time-dependent stochastic processes. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes a dual-domain fused long short-term memory (DDF-LSTM) model for efficient and accurate time-dependent reliability analysis. A novel network architecture is developed to jointly process information from both time-dependent and time-independent domains. Specifically, the time-independent variables are embedded into the initial hidden states, and a fully connected layer is introduced to map both LSTM outputs and time-independent variables into the final output space. Furthermore, an improved loss function is designed to emphasize the model's sensitivity to minimum responses, thereby improving the precision of failure probability estimation. The proposed method effectively captures the dependencies among random variables, stochastic processes, and the temporal behavior of limit state functions. Once trained, the DDF-LSTM model enables efficient Monte Carlo simulation to estimate time-dependent failure probabilities with minimal computational cost. Four case studies validate the proposed method's enhanced computational efficiency and predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [57] [Reliability Scales Inversely: Bigger Models Compound Mistakes Faster via a Hidden Auto-Regressive Risk Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18292)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大规模语言模型在自回归生成中的可靠性问题：模型规模增大虽提升能力，却加剧幻觉风险。通过与更强同族先验模型对比，量化逐位分歧 δ，将其二阶矩分解为偏差平方与风险方差，揭示规模扩大导致知识差距缩小但知识退化剧增，且模型在制造幻觉时表现出“自信但危险”的风险机制，这种机制具有因果性、自我延续性且难以被模型自身监测发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注知识缺口（数据、检索、规模），但忽略了自回归生成中模型因提前锁定低概率 token 而导致的错误雪球效应。作者旨在系统量化规模扩大对模型可靠性的负面影响，揭示幻觉产生的内在风险机制，并验证该机制的因果性及其逃避自我监测的特性。

Method: 引入同族更强的 Oracle 模型，计算逐位对数概率差 δ = log p_M - log p_O，并将其二阶矩精确分解为偏差项 KL(p_M || p_O)^2 与风险项 Var[δ]。在多个模型家族（如 14B 等规模）上进行实证分析，对比知识差距与知识退化的缩放规律，测量制造幻觉时刻的模型感知不确定性 H(p_M) 与 Oracle 参考风险的持久性差异，并通过固定 KL 散度的方差收缩干预验证因果性，同时评估基于 p_M 的检测器（如语义熵）在高风险分支上的失效率。

Result: 四大发现：(i) 随规模扩大，知识差距下降约 6 倍，但知识退化增长 11-39 倍；(ii) 幻觉发生时，模型感知不确定性快速下降，而 Oracle 风险持久高达 17 倍时长，形成“自信但危险”区间，连续幻觉概率在 14B 模型上增加 69%；(iii) 该风险区间具有因果性，固定 KL 的方差收缩干预可使三个模型家族的网页验证幻觉减少 35-74%；(iv) 该区间结构性逃避自我监测，仅基于 p_M 的检测器在高风险分支触发率降低约 30%（p<10^{-16}），而该分支包含近 4 倍的幻觉。

Conclusion: 更大模型通过一种主导、自我延续、因果且对模型自身不可见的失败模式，更快地将错误滚雪球般放大。单纯扩大规模无法解决可靠性问题，需针对自回归风险残差设计显式干预机制。

Abstract: As language models scale, answers start truer but degrade faster: scaling buys capability but erodes reliability. The knowledge-gap account - more data, retrieval, or scale - misses an auto-regressive risk residual that scale sharpens: the model commits to a low-probability token, conditions on it as established, and snowballs. We track this through per-position disagreement $δ= \log p_M - \log p_O$ against a stronger same-family oracle, whose second moment splits exactly into bias$^2$ $\mathrm{KL}(p_M \,\|\, p_O)^2$ and risk $\mathrm{Var}[δ]$. We present four findings: (i) under scaling, the knowledge gap falls $\approx$$6\times$ while knowledge degradation grows $11$-$39\times$; (ii) at a fabrication, felt uncertainty $H(p_M)$ relaxes quickly while oracle-referenced risk persists up to $17\times$ longer, leaving a confident-but-precarious risk regime that bridges consecutive fabrications ($+69\%$ at $14$B); (iii) this regime is causal - an on-policy, fixed-$\mathrm{KL}$ variance contraction cuts web-verified hallucination by $35$-$74\%$ across three model families; and, (iv) it structurally evades self-monitoring, with $p_M$-only detectors (e.g. semantic entropy) firing $\approx$$30\%$ less ($p<10^{-16}$) on the risky branch holding nearly $4\times$ more fabrications. Bigger models snowball mistakes faster, through a failure mode that is dominant, self-perpetuating, causal and invisible to the model itself.

</details>


### [58] [One Student, Many Teachers: Multi-Task On-Policy Distillation via Soft-Prompt Privileged Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18293)
*Yingzi Ma,Zichen Zhu,Ming Jiang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于软提示的在线策略自蒸馏方法（OPSD），通过冻结骨干网络仅训练软提示来构建任务特定教师，保持学生模型的表征几何结构，实现单任务和多任务下的高效知识蒸馏，且推理时丢弃提示。实验表明该方法在参数量极少的情况下达到或超越全量微调，多任务版本在保持通用能力的同时取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略自蒸馏教师要么在输入注入特权上下文导致事后合理化，要么通过微调权重导致跨任务漂移和灾难性遗忘。需要一种既能保持学生表征几何、又能高效适应多任务且不引入推理开销的教师构建方法。

Method: 提出 OPSD：教师仅通过可学习软提示与学生区别，在冻结骨干网络下于 (x, y_gold) 对训练提示，得到保持学生精确表征几何的任务特定教师。多任务扩展中，将合并语料库中每个样本路由至对应的软提示教师，单一学生并行吸收 K 个教师知识，推理时丢弃所有提示。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B-Base 和 Phi-4-mini-instruct 上跨四任务（科学、工具使用、生物、数学）实验：单任务版匹配或超越全量微调，训练参数量少数量级；多任务版在 Qwen3-1.7B-Base 上达最佳平均分 56.2，同时保持通用基准性能，而顺序 SFT 会双双下降。

Conclusion: 基于软提示的教师构建有效解决了表征漂移和灾难性遗忘问题，实现了参数高效、无推理开销的多任务知识蒸馏，为大模型持续技能获取提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) teaches large language models new skills through a teacher that shares the student's backbone and supervises its own rollouts. Existing teachers either inject privileged context at the input -- inducing post-hoc rationalization -- or fine-tune weights, accumulating drift and forgetting across tasks. We propose \method, whose teacher differs from the student only by a learnable soft prompt: trained on $(x, y_\text{gold})$ pairs with the backbone frozen, the prompt yields a task-specific teacher that preserves the student's exact representational geometry. \method\ extends naturally to multi-task settings by routing each example in a merged corpus to its corresponding soft-prompt teacher, allowing a single student to absorb knowledge from $K$ teachers in parallel; at inference, all prompts are discarded. On Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Phi-4-mini-instruct across four tasks (Science, Tool Use, Biology, Math), the single-task variant (OPD with a PT teacher) matches or exceeds full fine-tuning while training orders of magnitude fewer parameters, and the multi-task variant achieves the best overall average ($56.2$ on Qwen3-1.7B-Base) while preserving general-capability benchmarks -- in contrast to sequential SFT, which degrades both.

</details>


### [59] [Uncertainty Quantification for AI-Driven Crash Simulation Surrogates: A Comparative Study of Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensemble on Open-Source Bumper Beam Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18294)
*Sudeep Chavare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在 NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo 开源框架下，系统对比了 Monte Carlo Dropout（含 Concrete Dropout 自动学习 dropout 率）与 Deep Ensembles 两种不确定性量化方法，在汽车碰撞仿真钢制防撞梁基准上评估点精度、不确定性带校准度与计算成本，揭示了精度与校准度的根本权衡，证明了无超参数、校准良好的不确定性估计可在开源工程流程中以远低于集成方法的成本实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型在工程设计中越来越多地用于加速高保真仿真，但单一的点预测缺乏不确定性估计，无法指导工程师何时不应信任模型，这限制了其在安全关键工作流中的采用。因此需要系统比较主流不确定性量化方法，并解决 Monte Carlo Dropout 中 dropout 率需手动调节的常见批评。

Method: 基于 NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo 构建开源代理管线，采用 Concrete Dropout 在训练中端到端学习 dropout 率，消除手动超参数；对比 Monte Carlo Dropout 与 Deep Ensembles 两种 UQ 方法；以汽车碰撞仿真（钢制防撞梁冲击）为基准，在相同保留仿真数据上评估点预测精度、不确定性带校准度（如覆盖率、锐度）及训练/推理计算成本。

Result: 实验揭示了点预测精度与不确定性校准度之间的根本权衡：Deep Ensembles 虽然点精度略高，但校准度不如 Concrete Dropout，且计算成本高出数倍；Concrete Dropout 实现了无超参数、校准良好的不确定性估计，且计算成本仅为集成方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: 在开源工程工作流中，Concrete Dropout 提供了一种实用、低成本且校准良好的不确定性量化方案，挑战了 Deep Ensembles 作为默认黄金标准的假设，为安全关键仿真代理模型的可信部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogate models are increasingly being explored in engineering product development to augment simulation-driven design, offering near-instantaneous predictions that complement computationally expensive high-fidelity analyses. However, a critical gap limits their adoption in safety-critical workflows: a point prediction without an accompanying uncertainty estimate cannot tell an engineer when the model should not be trusted. This work presents a systematic, head-to-head comparison of two widely used uncertainty quantification approaches -- Monte Carlo Dropout and Deep Ensembles -- applied to an open-source surrogate pipeline built on NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo. A key contribution is the use of concrete dropout, a built-in PhysicsNeMo capability that eliminates the dropout rate as a manual hyperparameter by learning it end-to-end during training, directly addressing the most common criticism of Monte Carlo Dropout-based uncertainty quantification. Automotive crash simulation is used as the application domain, with a steel bumper beam impact problem serving as the benchmark. Both methods are evaluated on identical held-out simulations and compared on point accuracy, uncertainty band calibration, and computational cost. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between accuracy and calibration that challenges the common assumption that deep ensembles are the default gold standard for surrogate uncertainty quantification. The findings demonstrate that well-calibrated, hyperparameter-free uncertainty estimates are achievable within a fully open-source engineering workflow at a fraction of the computational cost of ensemble approaches.

</details>


### [60] [On the Limits of Support-Preserving Alignment and Bounded Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18295)
*Aryan Dutt,Rui Mao,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究结合有界安全过滤器的对齐方案是否能将大语言模型有害行为概率降至零，理论与实验均表明存在持久的有害输出下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究偏好对齐与外部过滤在实际流程中能否彻底消除有害行为，而非仅抑制显性形式。

Method: 形式化支持保持对齐算子与有界过滤算法（黑盒、白盒、统计查询访问），给出计算与信息论论证；在OpenRouter上对多款SOTA开源/托管LLM进行对抗提示词（网络安全场景、PKU-SafeRLHF）的实证评估。

Result: 有害输出率随过滤算力增加而下降，但始终稳定在零以上，存在持久的经验有害下限。

Conclusion: 在保持内部表示约束下，有界过滤无法完全消除基座模型分布支持的所有有害输出，存在理论与实证的根本限制。

Abstract: We study whether alignment schemes that reshape a base model's output distribution, combined with bounded safety filters, can drive the probability of harmful behavior to zero in modern large language models. Recent research suggests that harmful behaviors can persist under preference-based alignment and that external filtering can be computationally hard in the worst case, but it remains unclear whether practical alignment pipelines that largely preserve internal representations can eliminate harmful behavior entirely rather than merely suppressing its most visible forms. We formalize this setting using support-preserving alignment operators together with bounded filtering algorithms under black-box, white-box, and statistical-query access, and analyze their ability to approximate an ideal eliminator that removes all harmful mass. Building on this framework, we provide computational and information-theoretic arguments indicating that, under these constraints, bounded filtering may fail to eliminate all harmful outputs supported by the base model's distribution. To evaluate these limits empirically, we analyze a range of state-of-the-art open-weight and hosted LLMs accessed via OpenRouter under bounded black-box, white-box, and statistical-query filters on adversarial prompts drawn from curated cybersecurity scenarios and PKU-SafeRLHF.
